# W3a plan: booleans, STEP, the scheme family, polyhedra, diagnostics, ray tracing

Base: `origin/main` at `7e824be (plus the sm-rt-api freeze commits that add this plan)` (stage 0 and its polish merged). Rulings: decision 12 in `docs/design/w0/decisions.md`. Wave split: W3a is this plan; W3b (auto scheme selection, PyO3, viewer, DAGMC/msh4 export, primitives, surface-only `mesh.run`, `mesh.plan`, script recording, ANALYTIC/HIERARCHY ray tracing, the deferred backlog contract items) comes after.

Milestone (§4): the three-volume part (L-slab V1, triangle prism V2, box V3) is built by booleans or imported from STEP, imprinted and merged, meshed with submap/pave/trimesh/sweep/tetmesh, smoothed, checked with `mesh.validate`/`mesh.quality`, converted to polyhedra in a timeline fork, exported to `.smsh` 1.1, VTU, Exodus and OpenFOAM, and ray traced in process with the result checked against analytic chord lengths. CLI, MCP and the in-process path must give matching state hashes.

## 2. Tasks

### W3-BOOL. `sm-boolean` (L2, skeleton exists from stage 0): booleans, imprint, merge, webcut on the cell complex

- **Deps (decisions 9, 11, 12):** internal normal deps are sm-core, sm-math, sm-geom-api, sm-geom (`intersect_surfaces`, `curve_surface`, `curve_plane`, `SsiOptions`, `fit`), sm-topo-api (`TopologyEdit`, `FaceCut`, `validate`), sm-model-api (`Model` boundary tables, `ModelLayer`), sm-op-api, sm-layer-api (`topo_txn`, `commit_topology`, `MeshLayer` guard). External deps are rayon, serde, schemars and thiserror, and contract-crate dev-deps are allowed. Dev-only: sm-topo (`TopoStore`), sm-testkit, sm-mesh, sm-mesh-api, sm-scheme-api[stubs]. Mesh through `Driver::mesh` directly, as `crates/sm-scheme-api/tests/brick.rs` does. sm-model is a dev-dep for op-level tests only. It is an acyclic L2 peer already on main, which decision 12 allows. Do NOT depend on sm-mesher (L3) or on any W3a crate still being built. Cross-task fixtures come from sm-testkit, not from sibling crates.
- **Stage-0 items used by name (they exist or are being added; this task does not request them):**
  - `TopologyEdit::join_edges` / `join_faces`, implemented in CellComplex and TopoStore. Regularize uses them. There is no make_face/make_edge reconstruction fallback.
  - The sm-testkit boolean cases (`overlapping_bricks`, `brick_minus_cylinder`, `brick_with_cavity`, `partial_overlap_bricks`, `brick_sphere_half`, `crossing_cylinders`, `nurbs_cylinder_equiv`). Use them instead of local copies. Where a case is not yet there, build it in `tests/common` and say so in W3_REPORT.md.
  - The topology fixtures `nested_spheres` and `brick_with_void`.
  - The sm-boolean skeleton and workspace membership.
  - The concepts.yaml mapping (`boolean`, `imprint-merge` -> sm-boolean).
- **One crate, one kernel.** Every op is a composition of one fragment kernel:
  1. Candidate face pairs by AABB, sorted by `(id_a, id_b)`, evaluated with rayon and collected in index order.
  2. `sm_geom::intersect_surfaces` per pair (closed forms for plane/quadric, marched polylines otherwise), plus a private `cc.rs` curve-curve intersection (line/line, line/circle, circle/circle coplanar closed forms). sm-geom has only curve-surface. The NURBS part of cc.rs (Bezier-box subdivision + Newton) is stretch with M4.
  3. Clip each intersection curve to both faces (split points = boundary edges of A against face B and vice versa, tested with `classify_point_on_face`; midpoints of each sub-interval tested in both faces).
  4. Apply splits serially in sorted order: `make_vertex`, `split_edge`, `bridge_loops` for holed faces, then `split_face` with `Chord/Chain/ClosedLoop/ChainAt` as the topology dictates. `set_pcurve` on every new coedge: exact `Line2` for plane/cylinder/cone/sphere iso-lines, otherwise `Polyline2` from `MarchedCurve::pcurve_a/b` or from `ParametricSurface::project` samples.
  5. Coplanar or co-surface overlaps (`SsiResult::Coincident`) are imprinted as 2D region boundaries and the pair recorded with `record_coincidence`.
  6. Fragments are classified `In/Out/OnSame/OnOpposite` against the other operand by `locate_point`. It is a signed crossing count of a ray using `ParametricSurface::ray_hits_tol` and face-oriented normals, with a fixed list of 8 irrational directions tried in order. `On` is decided first by distance to the boundary. If all 8 directions are ambiguous the result is `Ambiguous{face, point}`.
  7. Volumes are assembled with `make_volume` from kept fragment uses (shells by flood fill across edges; Outer vs Void by the sign of the shell's signed volume from `face_moments().vector_area`). Originals are `kill_cascade`d (or kept, see `keep`). A face shared two-sided with a volume that is NOT in the op is never deleted: the survivor keeps its two-sided use and lineage. `record_split` / `record_merged` / `record_generated` declare lineage so `Model::apply_lineage` re-keys colors, groups, blocks and decks.
  8. Regularize merges coplanar/co-surface fragments with `join_faces` and dissolves degree-2 vertices with `join_edges`, with `Merged` lineage.
- **Public API (generic over `E: TopologyEdit + ?Sized` so tests run on `CellComplex` and `TopoStore`):**
  - `pub enum BoolRule { Unite, Subtract, Intersect }`; `pub struct BoolOptions { pub keep: bool, pub regularize: bool, pub merge: bool, pub max_gap: Option<f64> }`.
  - `pub fn boolean<E>(topo: &mut E, rule: BoolRule, a: &[EntityId], b: &[EntityId], o: &BoolOptions) -> Result<BoolOutcome, BooleanError>`, with `BoolOutcome { bodies, volumes, new_faces, faces: Vec<EntityId> }`. `a` and `b` are volume ids.
  - `pub fn imprint_bodies<E>(topo: &mut E, bodies: &[EntityId], o: &ImprintOptions) -> Result<ImprintOutcome, BooleanError>`.
  - `pub fn merge<E>(topo: &mut E, scope: &[EntityId], skip_faces: &[EntityId], o: &MergeOptions) -> Result<MergeReport, BooleanError>` and `pub fn merge_check(view: &dyn TopologyView, scope: &[EntityId], skip_faces: &[EntityId], o: &MergeOptions) -> MergeReport` (read-only). `skip_faces` is the explicit skip list; the library never sees `Model`, and the op fills it with the interface faces from the `Model` boundary tables. Skipped faces are reported (`status: skipped`, reason `Interface`), never merged.
    - Order is vertices, edges, faces. Candidate pairs are ascending `(min id, max id)`. The lowest id survives (TopologyEdit contract).
    - `MergeOptions { tol: Option<f64>, kinds: MergeKinds }`. `tol > ModelTolerance::linear_max` is `Invalid`.
  - `pub enum CutTool { Plane { origin: Point3, normal: Vector3 }, Surface { surface: SurfaceDef, window: Option<[f64; 6]> } }` is mandatory. The variants `Body(EntityId)` and `Sheet(EntityId)` are added with the stretch items (the enum is `#[non_exhaustive]`).
  - `pub fn webcut<E>(topo: &mut E, targets: &[EntityId], tool: &CutTool, o: &WebcutOptions) -> Result<WebcutOutcome, BooleanError>`. `WebcutOptions { merge: bool /*default true*/, imprint: bool, keep: KeepSide /*Both|Positive|Negative*/, consume_tool: bool }`. `WebcutOutcome { positive, negative, bodies, volumes, cut_faces }`. Plane and surface tools build cap faces from the chord loops (nested loops become inner loops) and call `split_volume`, exactly as `sm_topo_api::fixtures::webcut_brick` does.
  - `pub fn locate_point(view: &dyn TopologyView, volume: EntityId, p: Point3) -> Result<PointLocation, BooleanError>` (`In/On/Out`), public and documented as the reusable point-in-volume primitive.
  - `BooleanError` (thiserror) variants: `EmptyResult`, `Unsupported(&'static str)`, `NonManifoldInput(EntityId)`, `Ssi { a, b, source }`, `Tolerance { entity, needed, max }`, `Ambiguous { face, point }`, `ResultInvalid { volume, rule }`, `Topo(#[from] TopoError)`. Every message names the entities and a hint (raise `tol`, perturb the tool, intersect fewer bodies). After assembly each result volume is checked (`volume_moments > 0`, closed shells, `validate` on touched cells). A failure rolls the whole op back (`ResultInvalid`).
- **Plugin `BooleanPlugin` (id `"boolean"`; registers ops and queries only, never layers; assembly installs it with `CoreLayers`, which is integration work, not this task):**
  - `model.unite {bodies: [Body] (>=2, POSITIONAL "bodies"), keep?: bool=false, regularize?: bool=true, tol?: f64}`. Inputs that do not overlap or touch give `Invalid("bodies do not touch")` (proposed ruling, see Open). OUTPUTS: `bodies`(Many), `volumes`(Many), `new_faces`(Many; faces on intersection surfaces), `faces`(Many; remaining faces of the result volumes). A role lists only ids alive at the end, and an id is in one role only (`commit_topology` rule).
  - `model.subtract {targets: [Body], tools: [Body], keep?, regularize?, tol?}` has the same OUTPUTS.
  - `model.intersect {bodies: [Body] (>=2), keep?, regularize?, tol?}`. An empty result is `OpError::Failed` with the `EmptyResult` text and the state unchanged. `keep` keeps the inputs unchanged and bit-identical (the kernel works on a deep copy built with `make_*` that shares the curve/surface handles; document that a later `replace_surface` on one copy affects both).
  - `model.imprint {bodies: [Body] (>=2), merge?: bool=false, tol?: f64}`: every overlapping or intersecting face pair is imprinted. The `faces`/`curves` arguments belong to the stretch `imprint_curves`. OUTPUTS: `faces`(Many, the children), `edges`(Many, the new imprint edges).
  - `model.merge {entities?: [any topology cell], kinds?: ["vertex","edge","face"], tol?: f64, check_only?: bool=false}` (default scope all bodies). OUTPUTS: `vertices`, `edges`, `faces` (survivors, Many). Query `model.merge_check {entities?, tol?} -> MergeReport {pairs: [{kind, a, b, status: "mergeable"|"near"|"overlapping"|"conflict"|"skipped", gap, overlap_area?, hint}]}` (actionable, no mutation).
  - `model.webcut_plane {targets: [Body|Volume], plane: {axis:"x"|"y"|"z", offset} | {origin:[f64;3], normal:[f64;3]} | {vertices:[V;3]}, keep?: "both"|"positive"|"negative"="both", merge?: bool=true, imprint?: bool=false}`. A section = `keep` one half.
  - `model.webcut_surface {targets, face: Face, keep?, merge?, imprint?}` takes a planar face (its plane). `extended` for quadric faces is stretch.
  - `model.webcut_cylinder {targets, radius, axis: [f64;3], center: [f64;3], keep?, merge?, imprint?}`.
  - **Webcut OUTPUTS (all three):** `positive`(Many, bodies on the +normal side), `negative`(Many, bodies on the -normal side), `volumes`(Many, volumes of every piece), `cut_faces`(Many, `record_generated`), and `bodies`(Many, pieces that are on neither side; empty for these tools). Positive side = the side the tool surface normal points to (outward for a body tool). Milestone scripts use `positive`/`negative`. Roles are disjoint by the `commit_topology` rule, so `bodies` is the remainder, not the union (see Open). `merge=false` is Cubit `nomerge` (`unmerge_face` on each cap). Pieces become one body each (kill the old body, `make_body` per connected volume group, `record_split`).
  - **Common guards:** refuse volumes with a mesh (`ctx.layer::<MeshLayer>()`, missing layer tolerated, "mesh.delete first"), volumes that are decomposed or instanced in `Model`, and non-manifold inputs (baffle `Both` uses, fins): `Unsupported`. Every op uses `#[schemars(extend("x-entity-kind" = [...]))]` and `OUTPUTS`.

- **Acceptance (analytic; volumes/areas from `volume_moments`/`face_moments`, tolerance 1e-12 for planar, 1e-8 relative for curved; every result passes `sm_topo_api::validate` and `validate_geometry` with no issues, and `sm_model_api::validate` where a deck exists):**

Merge: the `two_bricks` equivalent merged by `model.merge` gives counts `[12,20,11,2,2]` (V,E,F,Vol,Body), Euler 3, total area 11, the shared face two-sided (left = lower volume id), `coincident_faces` status `Merged`, and lineage `Merged` on the dropped face. The canonical hash equals `sm_testkit::topo::merged_bricks()` when the bricks come from `model.create_brick`. If only record order differs, assert the structural list and say so in the report.
  2. Merge tolerance: bricks 1e-5 apart do not merge at the default. `merge_check` reports `near` with gap 1e-5 +-1e-12. `tol=2e-5` merges and records `Merged{gap}` = 1e-5. `tol > linear_max` is `Invalid`. A face in `skip_faces` (an interface face in the Model boundary table) stays unmerged and is reported `skipped`.

After imprint: 14 faces, both pairs `ImprintedNotMerged`. After merge: V16 E26 F13, Euler 3, shared face area 2, L-shaped remainders area 2 each, total face area 46.

     - `merge=false` gives 12 faces. `keep=positive` leaves one volume of 0.75, the cap as a boundary face, and an empty `negative`.

     - `webcut_surface` with a planar face lying in z=0.25 gives the same result as the plane.
  5. Webcut on quadrics:
     - Cylinder (r=1, h=2) at z=0.5 gives volumes pi*0.5 and pi*1.5, cap area pi, lateral split into 2*pi*0.5 and 2*pi*1.5.
     - Sphere r=1 at z=0 gives two 2*pi/3 volumes with cap area pi (ClosedLoop).

  6. Subtract:
     - 4^3 brick minus a z-axis cylinder r=1 (longer than the brick): volume 64-4*pi, area 96+6*pi, V10 E15 F7, Euler 2. Top/bottom faces carry an inner loop (area 16-pi). The lateral face normal points toward the axis.
     - Flush cylinder (coincident top/bottom planes, `OnSame`/`OnOpposite` path): identical result, no sliver faces.
     - Brick 10 minus an interior sphere r=2: volume 1000-32*pi/3, one volume with an Outer and a Void shell, V10 E13 F7 (degenerate edges excluded), Euler 4.

  8. Intersect: the two bricks of 7 give a 1x2x2 box (volume 4, area 16, V8 E12 F6). A cylinder r=1 inside a brick returns the cylinder (volume 4*pi, containment path, no intersection edges). Disjoint bricks fail with `EmptyResult` and the state hash unchanged.
  9. Quadric-quadric (marched, `crossing_cylinders` plus sphere/cylinder pairs): every intersection edge point lies on both surfaces within 1e-6. Inclusion-exclusion `V(A u B) = V(A) + V(B) - V(A n B)` holds within 1e-5 relative using our own three ops. Results validate clean.
  10. Property tests (proptest, integer-grid axis-aligned brick pairs 0..6 covering disjoint, touching, flush, contained, crossing): `V(A u B) = V(A)+V(B)-V(A n B)` and `V(A-B) = V(A)-V(A n B)` to 1e-12. `unite(A,B)` and `unite(B,A)` agree in volume, area and counts. Every result validates clean. Random planes through brick and cylinder: `V1+V2 = V`, cap area equals the analytic section.
  11. `keep=true` leaves the inputs' cells unchanged (canonical words of the old ids equal before/after) and the result ids are fresh. Without `keep` the inputs are in `Delta.retired` exactly. A failing op (Ambiguous, Tolerance, EmptyResult) leaves the state hash identical (atomic via `topo_txn`).
  12. Conformal mesh: `webcut_plane` a 4x2x2 brick at x=2 (merged), mesh both volumes in one `Driver::mesh` call with the stub `equal` + `sweep` schemes at size 1. Expect 16 hexes, 45 nodes (not 54), nodes on the cap classified on the shared `TopoFace`, every cap quad has a cell on each side, `sm_mesh_api::validate` empty, total volume 16. With `merge=false` the same mesh has 54 nodes (negative control).

Shared face with an outside volume (milestone path B): three bricks X=[0,1], Y=[1,2], Z=[2,3] (unit cross-section) are webcut/merged so X|Y and Y|Z are two-sided merged faces. `unite(X, Y)` gives one volume of 2. The Y|Z face is kept and stays two-sided between the united volume and Z (left/right uses intact, `Merged` lineage preserved, not deleted by `kill_cascade`). The X|Y seam face is dissolved by `join_faces`, and the coplanar side faces regularize to F6 for the union (V8 E12). Z is bit-identical (canonical words). The same holds for `subtract(Y; X)` where X is a target outside... precisely: `subtract(targets=[Y], tools=[X])` leaves the Y|Z face two-sided with Z unchanged. Also run with a color on Z and check it survives.
  14. Determinism: `sm_testkit::determinism::check` over the ops of 4, 6, 7, 12 and 13, comparing `ModelLayer` hashes for two runs, `RAYON_NUM_THREADS=1` against default, and concurrent runs. Cold replay from root with no `Diverged`. Ids of new cells follow the documented creation order (ascending pair order, then curve parameter).
  15. Failure modes are typed: tangent-only contact, a ray exactly through an edge with all 8 directions ambiguous, and an input with a mesh each return the documented `BooleanError` / `OpError`, never a panic, and leave the state hash unchanged.

A sheet that does not separate the target is `Invalid`. Also `imprint_curves<E>(topo, curves, faces, o)` with `model.imprint {faces?, curves?}`: curves project orthogonally onto the face surface and a curve further than `tol` from the surface is `Invalid`, never silently moved. Also `model.unmerge` (inverse of merge, uses `unmerge_vertex/edge/face`) and `model.webcut_surface {extended: true}` for quadric faces.

Stretch acceptance S3 (`nurbs_cylinder_equiv`): brick minus a NURBS surface cylinder equal to an analytic one has volume within 1e-4 relative, edge tolerance raised and `<= linear_max`, validate clean. A case that cannot be done returns a typed error naming faces, never a panic.
